I infer that every unveiling produces an aftereffect and re-enters a cycle; the final outcome may instead refer only to the punitive narrative.","changed_reading":{"before":"Day's unveiling is a complete, self-contained instant.","after":"Disclosure leaves a trace, entails an outcome, and recurs within a sequence whose next phase can cover the object again."}}],"surprising_valid_outliers":[{"outlier_id":"outlier_day_as_rebuke","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it activates a rebuke branch under the ordinary day noun. It remains anchored in the exact focus root and gains a coherent mechanism from unveiling, speech, message, and denial; downstream prose should present it only as a latent personification in which exposure admonishes, not as a replacement lexical gloss for day.","focus_anchor":"The day noun at word 1 carries the rebuke branch, and the word-3 verb makes its action an exposure of the feminine object.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ن ه ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001559","branch_id":"B004","role":"Harsh verbal driving-back personifies day as an admonishing agent."},{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B001","role":"Public uncovering gives the rebuke its nonverbal act: what was denied is made visible."},{"source_ref":"91:11","root":"ك ذ ب","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001290","branch_id":"B001","role":"Contradiction of truth supplies the human stance to which visible exposure can answer."},{"source_ref":"91:13","root":"ق و ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001272","branch_id":"B001","role":"Uttered speech supplies the explicit mode whose function daylight performs without words."},{"source_ref":"91:13","root":"ر س ل","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000563","branch_id":"B002","role":"Message-bearing turns the exposing day into an analogue of communicated warning."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Day simply makes her visible.","after":"Her exposure itself acts like a harsh public rebuke: daylight answers denial by forcing what was hidden into view."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_nuptial_display","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"The nuptial mechanism is branch-distant and should not make the referent literally a bride. It remains valid as an activation because the focus verb directly carries deliberate bridal presentation, the object is feminine, and later construction and covering branches add relational imagery; render it as ceremonial rhetoric of presentation only.","focus_anchor":"The word-3 transitive verb has a bridal-display branch and governs a feminine object suffix within the opening day.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ن ه ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001559","branch_id":"B002","role":"The opening public interval of day supplies the setting for display."},{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B003","role":"Deliberate bridal presentation directly supplies the ceremonial unveiling mechanism."},{"source_ref":"91:5","root":"ب ن ي","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000156","branch_id":"B006","role":"The construction root's marital-entry branch activates a nuptial relation beyond generic architecture."},{"source_ref":"91:4","root":"غ ش و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001088","branch_id":"B004","role":"The covering root's sexual-union branch supplies an intimate counter-image to daytime presentation."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Daylight generically exposes a feminine object.","after":"The clause can faintly stage a ceremonial unveiling in which day presents her into a relational field of beholding."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_predatory_gaze","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This cross-domain reading is surprising because it turns illumination into hunting optics. It remains anchored in the focus verb's searching-gaze branch and is independently activated by moonlit hunting, going out to hunt, and a bird-of-prey image; downstream prose should qualify the agency as an inferred predatory optics, not assert that day literally hunts.","focus_anchor":"The disclosure verb at word 3 has a branch of lifting a searching gaze in hunting imagery.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B008","role":"A lifted searching gaze supplies the focus-side scanning action."},{"source_ref":"91:2","root":"ق م ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001255","branch_id":"B003","role":"Pursuit or hunting under moonlight turns illumination into a condition for locating a target."},{"source_ref":"91:5","root":"س م و","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000745","branch_id":"B006","role":"Going out to hunt supplies an active seeking frame around the revealed object."},{"source_ref":"91:15","root":"ع ق ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001033","branch_id":"B013","role":"The bird of prey supplies the elevated searching perspective latent in the focus gaze branch."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Day is a neutral medium in which she happens to appear.","after":"Day can be carried as a searching optic that scans, finds, and exposes her as though locating quarry."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_burnished_blade","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"The blade mechanism selects the sharper half of a branch that also includes clearing eyesight, so it is not the default reading. It remains anchored in the focus polishing branch and gains material support from later cutting and wounding cues; render it as an analogy for sharpened discrimination, not literal weaponry in the sky.","focus_anchor":"The word-3 verb has a polishing branch that explicitly includes a blade as well as clarified sight.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B002","role":"Polishing a blade and clearing vision fuse optical clarity with a sharpened edge."},{"source_ref":"91:9","root":"ف ل ح","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001175","branch_id":"B001","role":"Cleaving and cutting supply the act performed by the sharpened distinction."},{"source_ref":"91:14","root":"ع ق ر","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001035","branch_id":"B001","role":"Wounding and overcoming preserve the dangerous edge of making a separation clear."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Day softly clarifies an indistinct scene.","after":"Day burnishes a cutting edge of perception, sharply separating forms and exposing the violence possible in clear distinctions."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_day_as_cleft","confidence":"medium","containment":"Treating day as a cleft rather than a substance is unusual but structurally economical: the focus roots supply widening and uncovering, while context supplies night, rupture, and a gap. It should be rendered as a spatial model of the day interval, not as a lexical claim that day means wound or gap.","focus_anchor":"The day root at word 1 carries opening and widening, and the word-3 verb gives that opening the result of manifestation.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ن ه ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001559","branch_id":"B003","role":"Widening until an open space appears supplies the geometry of a cleft."},{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B001","role":"Manifestation supplies what becomes possible through the aperture."},{"source_ref":"91:4","root":"ل ي ل","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001392","branch_id":"B001","role":"Night's contrary darkness supplies the enclosing material on both sides of the interval."},{"source_ref":"91:8","root":"ف ج ر","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001132","branch_id":"B001","role":"A broad rupture and release supply the event that opens the interval."},{"source_ref":"91:14","root":"ع ق ر","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001035","branch_id":"B014","role":"A gap between two things stabilizes the day as interval rather than luminous substance."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Day is a luminous substance spread over the world.","after":"Day is the recurrent cleft opened in night, an interval through which she becomes visible."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_stealth_snatch","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This activation is especially form-distant because it uses the day root's stealth-taking branch. It remains minimally coherent through the focus's abrupt transfer from hiddenness to manifestation and context images of deceptive mastery and false display; downstream prose should call it a sudden-snatch metaphor, never a direct gloss.","focus_anchor":"The day root at word 1 includes sudden or stealthy taking, while the word-3 verb supplies the thing taken: concealment from the feminine object.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ن ه ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001559","branch_id":"B006","role":"A sudden stealthy snatch recasts daylight as abruptly taking concealment away."},{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B001","role":"The resulting manifestation identifies concealment, rather than the object, as what was snatched."},{"source_ref":"91:2","root":"ق م ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001255","branch_id":"B007","role":"Mastery through gambling or deception supplies the risky, non-gentle manner of taking."},{"source_ref":"91:11","root":"ك ذ ب","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001290","branch_id":"B009","role":"False display supplies the concealment that the sudden daylight event strips away."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Visibility increases gradually as day spreads.","after":"Day can be imagined as suddenly snatching away the cover on which a deceptive appearance depended."}},{"outlier_id":"outlier_embodied_fear","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"Moving from cosmic visibility to affect on a body is cross-domain. It remains anchored because the focus verb includes manifestation and open notoriety while the context fear branch explicitly concerns fear becoming visible on a person; qualify it as an analogy for externalized interiority, not the primary object-reference.","focus_anchor":"The word-3 verb makes hidden states manifest and can make their bearer openly known.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B001","role":"Manifestation after hiddenness supplies the transition from inward affect to outward sign."},{"source_ref":"91:3","root":"ج ل و","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000256","branch_id":"B006","role":"Open notoriety makes the once-private condition publicly readable."},{"source_ref":"91:7","root":"ن ف س","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001533","branch_id":"B013","role":"The mind and inward apprehension supply the interior whose condition may surface."},{"source_ref":"91:15","root":"خ و ف","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000447","branch_id":"B005","role":"Fear visibly appearing on a person supplies the embodied disclosure mechanism."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Day reveals only external cosmic objects.","after":"Daylight can also figure the moment an inward condition writes itself visibly onto a living bearer."}}],"summary":{"strongest_changes":["The neighboring sun and night clauses turn the focus into an active uncovering of a likely solar object and the exact reversal of nocturnal covering.","River, widening, rupture, ploughing, overflow, and watering cues make daylight a material flow that cuts, irrigates, and sometimes inundates a field of visibility.","Construction, spreading, and completion recast disclosure as a world-making operation rather than a passive clock interval.","Moral concealment, denial, speech, and message make visibility evidentiary and communicative without guaranteeing truthful interpretation.","Later cutting, eradication, and equalization keep a disturbing co-reading alive in which making clear can involve displacement or violent clearance."],"what_was_special":"The focus ayah sustained several irreducible mechanisms at once: ordinary solar unveiling, reversible cover removal, spatial aperture, hydraulic flow, polished perception, public signification, cultivated growth, and exploratory ceremonial, admonitory, predatory, and destructive presentations. The most productive surprise came from allowing the day root's non-day branches and the disclosure root's display, expulsion, polishing, and gaze branches to remain active under context rather than collapsing them into generic brightness.","remaining_uncertainties":["The feminine object most naturally tracks the sun in the immediate sequence, but the whole visible field, the earth, or a deliberately unresolved object remain possible.","The exact amount of causal force in the sequence is uncertain: many arrows from context branch to focus mechanism are abductive analogies rather than grammatical entailments.","Non-dominant mapped roots produce valuable legibility and overflow activations, but their distance requires explicit qualification.","Ceremonial, predatory, rebuking, stealth-taking, and violent-clearance readings are coherent latent overlays, not proposed replacements for the ordinary daytime sense.","Visibility may disclose truth, stage a manipulable appearance, or merely supply evidence; the packet keeps all three epistemic readings live."]}}}},"v12_reader_walks":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Day as active unveiling, not merely time.** Lexical evidence: ن ه ر B002 \"day opening with light\"; ج ل و B001 \"uncovering and manifestation\"; ج ل و B007 \"daylight and clear sky.\" Structural cue: the verb جلّى intensifies the noun نهار, and this sits between moon-following and night-covering. Assigned role: day performs disclosure. Abductive move: I supply the causal arrow that daylight removes a cover from the feminine referent; the packet supplies day/opening/uncovering branches and the later cover root غ ش و. Reading change: the ayah becomes an event of revelation, a functional counterpart to both divine inspiration and public exposure of wrongdoing.\n\n2. **Unveiling as bride-like presentation of the hidden object.** Lexical evidence: ج ل و B003 \"bridal display\"; ر ح م B002/B003 kinship/womb in 91:0; ن ف س B011/B013 soul-self and inner mind in 91:7. Structural cue: repeated `ها` links the cosmic object across sun, moon, day, night, sky, earth, soul. Assigned role: the bridal-display variant makes manifestation relational and guarded, not just optical. Abductive move: I infer a transferred social scene: something is being presented for recognition; the packet supplies the repeated feminine pronoun and the display branch. Reading change: جلّىها can be heard as a formal unveiling of a reality that will later be internalized as the self's disclosed moral condition.\n\n3. **River/opening branch makes disclosure a channeling of flow.** Lexical evidence: ن ه ر B001 \"river cutting the ground with flowing water\"; ن ه ر B003 \"opening or widening until something flows\"; ف ج ر B001 \"wide splitting and outflow\" in 91:8; س ق ي B003 \"irrigation share and channel\" in 91:13. Structural cue: day/unveiling occurs before fissure/outflow and watering rights. Assigned role: نهار is activated as open channel, making disclosure a path for flow. Abductive move: I supply an ecological analogy between light opening and water channels opening; the packet supplies river/opening, fajr-splitting, and watering-channel branches. Reading change: the ayah is not only visual; it also becomes hydraulic, opening channels through which life or breach will later run.","retrospective_surprises_md":"91:13's سُقياها and 91:14's بذنبهم made the river/opening branch of ن ه ر more active than a simple day/night contrast. The later water-right shows that disclosure is also channeling: what opens must be rightly apportioned, and when the channel/right is violated the Lord's leveling answers it.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s091/full_context_control/reader_s091_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_reader_walks_wide":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Day as an active disclosure operation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ه ر/B002` is day opening with light and fixed `ج ل و/B001` is uncovering after concealment; 91:4 answers with `غ ش و/B001`, a covering veil. **Structural cue and role:** the matched constructions `إذا جلاها` and `إذا يغشاها` reverse one another around the same suffix, assigning day the role of disclosure rather than background time. **Abductive move:** the grammatical reversal is explicit; treating the two clauses as opposed operations on one visible field is inferred. **Reading change:** day does not merely coincide with visibility; it actively makes the referent manifest.\n\n2. **Perceptual polishing before inner discrimination.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ج ل و/B002` is polishing a blade or clearing sight; 91:7 `ن ف س/B013` is inner mind and discernment, followed in 91:8 by the paired contents given to it. **Structural cue and role:** outward clearing precedes the shaping and informing of an inward perceiver, so polishing serves as a material model for legibility. **Abductive move:** the packet gives outer disclosure followed by inner discrimination; the arrow from clarified perception to readable moral distinction is analogical and inferred. **Reading change:** `جلاها` acquires the force of sharpening the capacity to see, not only increasing illumination.\n\n3. **A controlled opening that exposes the risk of breach.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ه ر/B003` is opening or widening until something flows; 91:8 `ف ج ر/B001` is a wide split and outflow, alongside `ف ج ر/B004`, breach of restraint. **Structural cue and role:** the focus window moves from day's opening to a self informed of `فجورها`, linking optical aperture to moral rupture. **Abductive move:** the two opening-images are lexical data; positing controlled disclosure and uncontrolled breach as two outcomes of aperture is inferred. **Reading change:** day becomes a channel of manifestation whose mechanics also make the later danger of boundary-breaking intelligible.","retrospective_surprises_md":"91:7-8 shifted the disclosure mechanism inward: a formed discerning self receives opposed contents. 1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.
4. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing — and end it with the density audit required by the task document. ## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
